Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any tokenization module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 417. The projective resolution has length at most 485 with Betti numbers bounded by 2.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by tokenization generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 417, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 485. QED.
